## Declared blind spots

- `*.**` (mask /Expires \d{2}/\d{4}/): the test card's expiry is the recording month plus one year: it depends on the day a fixture was recorded, not on the probe
- `*.layout.**` (tolerance 16): layout boxes compared within 16 px: font rendering moves the same page by up to 12 px between platforms
- `*.thumbnail` (perceptual, reported above 2 % of cells, never scored): pixels are reported, never scored: a 64x40 grey thumbnail with iframes masked, 0.2 % apart between platforms
- `dev-ag-cancel-now-delete`: recorded noise on cancel.body.items.data[0].plan.created, cancel.body.items.data[0].price.created, cancel.body.plan.created
- `dev-ag-coupon-discounts`: recorded noise on apply.body.items.data[0].plan.created, apply.body.items.data[0].price.created, apply.body.plan.created, read.body.discounts[0].source.coupon, read.body.items.data[0].plan.created, read.body.items.data[0].price.created, read.body.plan.created
- `dev-ag-downgrade-no-proration`: recorded noise on update.body.items.data[0].plan.created, update.body.items.data[0].price.created, update.body.plan.created
- `dev-ag-reads`: recorded noise on customer.body.invoice_prefix, price.body.created, subscriptions.body.data[0].items.data[0].plan.created, subscriptions.body.data[0].items.data[0].price.created, subscriptions.body.data[0].plan.created
